A gateway-only hub object carries its gateway Pod count solely to preserve the legacy v1beta1 Scale projection; v1beta2 gateway-only Scale updates are rejected.
status.scaleSelector <string>	# ScaleSelector is the serialized exact selector for the Pods counted by ScaleReplicas. It normally selects the default storage group; on a gateway-only hub object it selects the gateway Pods for legacy v1beta1 Scale reads. It is kept separate from the aggregate Selector so hidden workloads cannot make /scale fail to converge.
status.scrubStatus <object>	# ScrubStatus contains the status of data scrub operations. Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
status.scrubStatus.corruptedBlocks <integer> format=int32	# CorruptedBlocks is the number of corrupted blocks found in the last scrub.
status.scrubStatus.lastCompleted <string> format=date-time	# LastCompleted is when the last scrub completed.
status.scrubStatus.nextRun <string> format=date-time	# NextRun is when the next scrub is scheduled to run.
status.scrubStatus.nodeStatuses <[]object>	# NodeStatuses contains per-node scrub status.
status.scrubStatus.nodeStatuses[].errorsFound <integer> format=int32	# ErrorsFound is the number of errors found on this node.
status.scrubStatus.nodeStatuses[].itemsChecked <integer> format=int64	# ItemsChecked is the number of items checked.
status.scrubStatus.nodeStatuses[].nodeId <string>	# NodeID is the node identifier.
status.scrubStatus.nodeStatuses[].progress <integer>	# Progress percentage (0-100).
status.scrubStatus.nodeStatuses[].running <boolean>	# Running indicates if scrub is running on this node.
status.scrubStatus.paused <boolean>	# Paused indicates if the scrub is paused.
status.scrubStatus.progress <string>	# Progress is a human-readable progress description.
status.scrubStatus.running <boolean>	# Running indicates if a scrub is currently running on any node.
status.scrubStatus.tranquilityLevel <integer>	# TranquilityLevel is the current tranquility setting.
status.selector <string> (required)	# Selector is the aggregate serialized label selector for pods managed by this cluster. It is not used by the scale subresource.
status.stagedLayoutVersion <integer> format=int64	# StagedLayoutVersion is the staged layout version pending application. Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
status.stagedRoles <integer> format=int32	# StagedRoles is the number of roles in the staged layout. Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
status.storageDrain <object>	# StorageDrain records the single cluster-wide positive-capacity removal and block-migration proof. It excludes every other layout mutation until the exact actor completes its Kubernetes handoff.
status.storageDrain.actor <object> (required)	# Actor is the exact object authorized to advance this transaction.
status.storageDrain.actor.apiVersion <string> (required)	# APIVersion is the actor's Kubernetes API version.
status.storageDrain.actor.kind <string> (required) enum=GarageCluster|GarageNode	# Kind is GarageCluster for a cluster-wide Drain deletion or GarageNode for one logical storage member's permanent role removal.
status.storageDrain.actor.name <string> (required)	# Name is the actor's Kubernetes object name.
status.storageDrain.actor.namespace <string> (required)	# Namespace is the actor's Kubernetes namespace.
status.storageDrain.actor.uid <string> (required)	# UID is the immutable Kubernetes UID of the actor incarnation.
status.storageDrain.completedAt <string> format=date-time	# CompletedAt is set only after exact repair workers completed cleanly and every enabled block-resync worker passed the terminal safety checks. The transaction remains active until Actor completes its Kubernetes handoff.
status.storageDrain.errorCount <integer> (required) format=int64	# ErrorCount is the aggregate number of block resync errors.
status.storageDrain.layoutVersion <integer> (required) format=int64	# LayoutVersion is the current Garage layout version observed with these verification processes and queue counters.
status.storageDrain.managedPodUids <map[string]string>	# ManagedPodUIDs fingerprints the exact locally managed Garage processes observed when this proof revision started. Any pod replacement invalidates worker-ID baselines because Garage worker counters can restart from zero. Keys are full Garage node IDs and values are Kubernetes Pod UIDs.
status.storageDrain.queueLength <integer> (required) format=int64	# QueueLength is the observed aggregate block-resync queue depth. It is informational: Garage also queues future garbage collection, so readiness is based on completed repair workers and idle resync workers instead.
status.storageDrain.quietSince <string> format=date-time	# QuietSince starts after every exact repair worker completes without errors and resync-worker error baselines are recorded. The operator waits through Garage's maximum known delayed-resync interval, then requires every exact resync worker idle and error-free before a process may be stopped.
status.storageDrain.removedStorageNodeIds <[]string> (required)	# RemovedStorageNodeIDs is the sorted positive-capacity subset whose removal requires block-migration proof. Recording it before Apply keeps the safety gate durable across controller crashes and Admin API failures.
status.storageDrain.repairBaselines <map[string]integer>	# RepairBaselines records each storage node's highest worker ID before the operator launches a transaction-specific Blocks repair. It is persisted before launch so a crash cannot lose ownership of the resulting worker.
status.storageDrain.repairWorkerIds <map[string]integer>	# RepairWorkerIDs records the exact post-baseline Blocks repair worker whose clean completion was observed on each source and destination process in VerificationNodeIDs.
status.storageDrain.requiresEmptyQueue <boolean> (required)	# RequiresEmptyQueue is true when at least one verification process is not proven to use this GarageCluster's authoritative RPC timeout. In that case the normally informational future-work queue must also become empty.
status.storageDrain.resyncErrorBaselines <map[string]integer>	# ResyncErrorBaselines records the cumulative error counter of every exact enabled block-resync worker after all repair scans complete. Any increase restarts the repair transaction.
status.storageDrain.roleRemovalNodeIds <[]string> (required)	# RoleRemovalNodeIDs is the sorted set of every exact Garage layout role this actor is authorized to remove, including capacityless gateway roles in a unified cluster Drain.
status.storageDrain.startedAt <string> (required) format=date-time	# StartedAt records when the pre-Apply removal intent became durable.
status.storageDrain.targetHash <string> (required)	# TargetHash fingerprints both normalized target sets. Every status update compares both transactionId and targetHash so a stale reconcile cannot overwrite a newer target revision owned by the same actor.
status.storageDrain.transactionId <string> (required)	# TransactionID distinguishes separate drains owned by the same object UID.
status.storageDrain.unavailableSourceNodeIds <[]string>	# UnavailableSourceNodeIDs is the sorted subset of removedStorageNodeIds whose processes and local disks were explicitly acknowledged as permanently lost. Those sources cannot run the normal Blocks scan; the terminal proof instead covers every surviving positive-capacity destination after an administrator removes the exact dead role through Garage's recovery workflow. Data present only on a listed source may be lost.
status.storageDrain.verificationNodeIds <[]string>	# VerificationNodeIDs is the sorted union of removed source processes and current positive-capacity destinations covered by this transaction's completed block-repair scans.
status.storageReadyReplicas <integer> format=int32	# StorageReadyReplicas is the aggregate number of storage identities that are connected and committed in Garage's layout.
status.storageReplicas <integer> format=int32	# StorageReplicas is the aggregate desired/current storage identity count across the default group, ordinary Manual GarageNodes, and node-local pools. Use ScaleReplicas for the narrow Kubernetes Scale projection.
status.storageRollout <object>	# StorageRollout is the controller-owned durable record for the single managed storage/gateway pod currently being replaced. Nil when no parent-controlled rollout handoff is active.
status.storageRollout.clusterGeneration <integer> format=int64	# ClusterGeneration is the most recent recovery-safe GarageCluster spec generation published into this actor's desired workload template.
status.storageRollout.desiredConfigHash <string> (required)	# DesiredConfigHash is the rendered Garage configuration revision.
status.storageRollout.desiredPodSpecHash <string> (required)	# DesiredPodSpecHash is the operator-computed pod template revision.
status.storageRollout.garageNodeGeneration <integer> format=int64	# GarageNodeGeneration is the exact actor's most recently published workload-safe spec generation, including the generated GarageNode behind a node-local-pool actor.
status.storageRollout.garageNodeId <string> (required)	# GarageNodeID is the exact Garage identity reported by the selected actor before its Pod is deleted. A replacement must rediscover this same identity; a new ID under the same Kubernetes objects is never accepted as a handoff.
status.storageRollout.garageNodeName <string>	# GarageNodeName identifies a StatefulSet-backed PVC, SMB, or gateway actor. Mutually exclusive with NodeLocalPoolName and KubernetesNodeName.
status.storageRollout.garageNodeUid <string> (required)	# GarageNodeUID anchors the actor to one immutable GarageNode incarnation. It is recorded for StatefulSet and node-local-pool actors alike.
status.storageRollout.kubernetesNodeName <string>	# KubernetesNodeName identifies the Kubernetes Node represented by a node-local-pool actor.
status.storageRollout.kubernetesNodeUid <string>	# KubernetesNodeUID anchors a node-local-pool actor to the Kubernetes Node incarnation whose HostPath storage was validated. Empty for StatefulSets.
status.storageRollout.nodeLocalPoolName <string>	# NodeLocalPoolName identifies a spec.storage.nodeLocalPools entry.
status.storageRollout.persistentVolumeClaims <[]object>	# PersistentVolumeClaims anchors a StatefulSet-backed actor to every exact PVC mounted by the Pod selected for replacement. Recreated/deleting claims fail closed so controller-incarnation recovery cannot bind empty storage or silently create a new Garage identity under a reused claim name. Empty for node-local-pool HostPath actors.
status.storageRollout.persistentVolumeClaims[].name <string> (required)	# Name is the namespaced PVC referenced by the selected Pod.
status.storageRollout.persistentVolumeClaims[].uid <string> (required)	# UID is the exact Kubernetes PVC incarnation that contains the actor's metadata/data at selection time.
status.storageRollout.previousPodUid <string> (required)	# PreviousPodUID is persisted before the OnDelete pod is deleted. Recovery never accepts this UID as the completed replacement.
status.storageRollout.recoveryPodName <string>	# RecoveryPodName and RecoveryPodUID identify the one failed replacement durably selected for retry deletion. They are written before DELETE and retained until that exact UID is absent, making the operation idempotent across manager and API failures. Both are empty when no retry deletion is pending.
status.storageRollout.recoveryPodUid <string>
status.storageRollout.recoveryRequest <string>	# RecoveryRequest is the last recover-storage-rollout annotation nonce the controller durably consumed before deleting a failed replacement again.
status.storageRollout.retiredWorkloadUids <[]string> max=32	# RetiredWorkloadUIDs records workload-controller incarnations that were replaced behind a scheduling fence. Late Pods from these exact UIDs remain identifiable without trusting reused names; node-local-pool recovery waits for them to disappear rather than deleting healthy non-candidate members. Recovery fails closed before a 33rd retired controller incarnation so this exact old-Pod exclusion set remains bounded in parent status.
status.storageRollout.statefulSetWorkloadRecreationSafe <boolean>	# StatefulSetWorkloadRecreationSafe records that the selected StatefulSet's volumeClaimTemplate retention policy does not delete claims when the StatefulSet is deleted. It is false for node-local-pool actors and causes missing StatefulSet recovery to fail closed when claim retention was unsafe.
status.storageRollout.workloadFenced <boolean>	# WorkloadFenced is true after a replacement StatefulSet/DaemonSet UID was CAS-adopted but before that controller was allowed to schedule its first Pod (replicas=0 for StatefulSet, impossible nodeSelector for DaemonSet).
status.storageRollout.workloadUid <string> (required)	# WorkloadUID is the exact StatefulSet or DaemonSet controller incarnation authorized to create and replace this actor's Pod.
status.storageStats <object>	# StorageStats contains cluster-wide storage statistics.
status.storageStats.availableCapacity <int-or-string>	# AvailableCapacity is the available storage across all nodes.
status.storageStats.healthyPartitions <integer> format=int32	# HealthyPartitions is the number of partitions with full redundancy.
status.storageStats.totalCapacity <int-or-string>	# TotalCapacity is the total storage capacity across all nodes.
status.storageStats.totalPartitions <integer> format=int32	# TotalPartitions is the total number of partitions in the layout.
status.storageStats.usedCapacity <int-or-string>	# UsedCapacity is the used storage across all nodes.
status.totalNodes <integer>	# TotalNodes is the total nodes across all clusters (local + remote). Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
status.unreachablePeers <[]string>	# UnreachablePeers lists peers that have been continuously down beyond the sustained-unreachable threshold, each as "<shortNodeId> (down <duration>)". Drives the PeerUnreachable condition. Empty when all peers are reachable.
status.workerCount <integer> format=int32	# WorkerCount is the total number of background workers. Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
status.workers <object>	# Workers contains detailed information about background workers. Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
status.workers.busy <integer> format=int32	# Busy is the number of busy/active workers.
status.workers.errored <integer> format=int32	# Errored is the number of workers with errors.
status.workers.errors <[]object>	# Errors contains details about worker errors.
status.workers.errors[].consecutiveErrors <integer> format=int32	# ConsecutiveErrors is the count of consecutive errors.
status.workers.errors[].lastError <string>	# LastError is the last error message.
status.workers.errors[].lastErrorSecsAgo <integer> format=int64	# LastErrorSecsAgo is seconds since the last error.
status.workers.errors[].name <string>	# Name is the worker name.
status.workers.errors[].workerId <integer> format=int64	# WorkerID is the worker identifier.
status.workers.idle <integer> format=int32	# Idle is the number of idle workers.
status.workers.total <integer> format=int32	# Total is the total number of background workers.
status.workers.variables <map[string]string>	# Variables contains runtime worker configuration variables.
status.workersFailed <integer> format=int32	# WorkersFailed is the number of failed workers. Deprecated: never populated by the operator. It will be removed in the next API version.
